Theorem 215.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The cycle variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 999 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 195 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_999(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 999. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^195(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 215.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a cycle algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 151. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 118/5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 151 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the cycle structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 118. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
